- **Summary**: 针对现有AI科研系统孤立运作的问题，提出爱因斯坦竞技场，一个智能体原生平台，支持开放分布式研究，包含验证器、排行榜和讨论论坛。智能体通过集体交互发现了12个新的最优结果，如第11维接吻数从593提升至604。

<h3 id="192">[192] FOGO: Forgetting-aware Orthogonalization Optimizer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10406](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10406) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Toan Nguyen, Yang Liu, Trung Le, Celso de Melo, Flora D. Salim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Continual Learning, Catastrophic Forgetting
- **Summary**: 针对遗忘不仅是持续学习问题，而是普遍存在的优化现象（主导梯度抑制稀有但有用的更新方向），提出FOGO优化器，通过谱正交化动量更新和紧凑码本记忆解决梯度冲突。在类别不平衡分类、持续学习、LLaVA微调和GPT-2预训练中一致提升收敛和知识保留。

<h3 id="193">[193] Vision-Assisted Foundation Model for Solving Multi-Task Vehicle Routing Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10431](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10431) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuangchun Gui, Zhiguang Cao, Wen Song, Yew-Soon Ong
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Combinatorial Optimization, Vehicle Routing Problem
- **Venue**: TNNLS
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有多任务车辆路径问题求解器仅使用图模态，难以处理具有多种约束的变体的问题。提出了一种视觉辅助基础模型（VaFM），通过卷积神经网络编码包含所有约束的输入图像，并将图像块嵌入与图节点融合以生成解，同时设计辅助任务解决像素不平衡问题。在16种VRP变体上的实验表明，VaFM优于现有方法，尤其在复杂约束变体上表现突出。

<h3 id="194">[194] Mitigating Bias in Low-SNR Financial Reinforcement Learning via Quantum Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10448) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zeyu Liu, Xuanzhi Feng, Sing Kwong Lai, Yuanchen Gao, Xiaoyi Pang, Hualei Zhang, Jingcai Guo, Jie Zhang, Song Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Financial AI, Quantum Machine Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ZeyuLIU-UST/FPQC-SAC-main)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对金融领域低信噪比环境下软演员-评论家（SAC）方法不稳定的问题，提出FPQC-SAC，一种在演员和评论家网络前放置紧凑有界参数化量子电路（PQC）的SAC变体，以在表示层面约束特征传播。实验表明，在真实世界投资组合管理任务中，FPQC-SAC相比标准SAC累计收益相对提升66.89%，优于最佳深度强化学习基线约27%。

<h3 id="195">[195] The Distributed Detectability Band Against Marginal-Preserving Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10456](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10456) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhang Qinqin, Gao Yuze
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 该论文针对AI监控中每个步骤的阈值检测可能被跨越多个步骤的分布式破坏攻击绕过的问题，构建了一种基于高斯copula AR(1)的边缘保持、相关性编码的分布式破坏攻击。实验表明，基于分布形状的监控器（Monitor A）被完全击败（AUC 0.52，随机水平），而基于时序相关性的监控器（Monitor B）在1%假正率下AUC达到0.79-0.97，证明存在非空的可检测区间。

<h3 id="196">[196] Minimum Distortion Quantization with Specified Output Distribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10458](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10458) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aolin Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI; math.OC; math.ST
- **Tags**: Quantization, Information Theory
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了在指定输出分布条件下，最小化估计均方误差的最优量化器设计。推导出最优量化器形式为X=σ(F_{σ^{-1}(X)}^{-1}(F_W(W)))，其中σ是最优排列。当输入或输出分布均匀时，量化器简化为X=F_X^{-1}(F_W(W))。该量化器可用于控制输出熵、最大化互信息、匹配信道输入要求或数据匿名化。

<h3 id="197">[197] LakeQA: An Exploratory QA Benchmark over a Million-Scale Data Lake</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10460](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10460) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haonan Wang, Jiaxiang Liu, Yurong Liu, Austin Senna Wijaya, Tianle Zhou, Eden Wu, Yijia Chen, Wanting You, Reya Vir, Daniela Pinto, Grace Fan, Yusen Zhang, Juliana Freire, Eugene Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有问答基准缺乏在大型数据湖中进行搜索和推理的综合评估的问题，提出了LakeQA基准，基于约9.5TB的异构文本资源（维基百科和政府开放数据），每个样本由至少一位博士级专家标注，需要多跳推理。实验表明，GPT-5.2在LakeQA上的精确匹配得分仅为18.37%，说明该基准具有挑战性。

<h3 id="198">[198] ERAlign: Energy-based Representation Alignment of GNNs and LLMs on Text-attributed Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10461](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10461) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xianlin Zeng, Fan Xia, Xiangyu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Representation Learning, Energy-based Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对文本属性图上图神经网络与大型语言模型表示对齐不足的问题，提出基于能量模型的表示对齐框架ERAlign，通过层间距离度量并以能量模型目标优化，实现分布一致性。引入能量差异避免高采样成本。在8个TAG数据集上，ERAlign在不同监督程度和跨任务迁移场景下均达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="199">[199] UPLOTS: A Unified Pretrained Language Model for Constrained Time-series Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10466](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10466) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Du Yin, Hao Xue, Jinliang Deng, Yang Yang, Shuang Ao, Arian Prabowo, Flora Salim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Generation, Language Model, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有时间序列生成方法为每个数据集单独训练模型、缺乏可扩展性的问题，提出UPLOTS，一个统一的提示引导语言模型框架，通过动态多数据集损失重加权和提示-模式映射，实现跨领域的约束时间序列生成。在四个真实基准和多种约束设置下评估，并展示了对未见约束组合和下游预测任务的泛化能力。

<h3 id="200">[200] Detecting Speculative Language in Biomedical Texts using Recurrent Neural Tensor Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10471](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10471) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dhruv Dixit
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text Classification, Biomedical NLP, Speculative Language Detection
- **Summary**: 该论文针对生物医学文本中推测性语言的自动检测问题，使用分布式句子表示和深度学习技术，比较了段落向量模型和递归神经张量网络（RNTN）等方法。实验发现RNTN的F1=0.885略优于线性bigram SVM的0.881，而段落向量模型仅0.368。讨论了性能差异因素并给出未来研究方向。

<h3 id="201">[201] Decoupling Thought from Speech: Knowledge-Grounded Counterfactual Reasoning for Resilient Multi-Agent Argumentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10475) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jakub Masłowski, Jarosław A. Chudziak
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Reasoning, Multi-Agent Argumentation
- **Venue**: KES 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多智能体辩论框架在长时间交互中可能出现的逻辑退化、论点重复和角色漂移问题，提出知识基础的反事实推理（KG-CFR），一种将私有检索增强规划缓冲与公共执行层分离的双阶段架构。在动态资源分配环境下，KG-CFR在超过95%的扰动运行中防止了关键质量下降，并将整体论点质量从0.694提升至0.822。

<h3 id="202">[202] Advancing the State-of-the-Art in Empirical Privacy Auditing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10481](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10481) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicole Mitchell, Galen Andrew, Arun Ganesh, Brendan McMahan, Peter Kairouz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CR; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Privacy, LLM Memorization, Membership Inference Attack
- **Summary**: 该论文针对大型语言模型参数高效微调中的记忆化问题，提出通过高温采样生成合成金丝雀示例，用于经验隐私审计。这些非私有金丝雀具有高可识别性，并能重复插入而不危及真实数据隐私。还引入了一种基于微调辅助模型在合成数据上的强大审计方法。系统研究了模型容量和金丝雀熵对记忆化的交互影响。

<h3 id="203">[203] Stop Early, Spend Less: Hidden-State Probes as a Practical Recipe for Streaming Moderation of LLM Outputs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10487](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10487) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huizhen Shu, Xuying Li, Piao Xue
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Model Efficiency, Hidden-State Probe
- **Summary**: 该论文针对大语言模型输出安全过滤效率低的问题，提出在模型隐藏状态上训练轻量级令牌级探针，实现流式安全检测。探针重用生成器的激活，无需额外前向传播，可在解码循环内进行亚毫秒级令牌级安全检查。实验表明，单层中间探针可恢复强防护模型的大部分决策，相比后处理模型大幅降低计算开销。

<h3 id="204">[204] Achieving Cloud-Grade SLOs for Local Mixture-of-Experts Inference through CPU-GPU Hybrid Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10493](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10493) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenxin Wang, Yule Hou, Yu Ji, Peng Qu, Youhui Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Inference, Hardware Acceleration
- **Venue**: OSDI '26
- **Summary**: 该论文针对本地部署大混合专家（MoE）模型服务质量不足的问题，提出了CPU-GPU混合系统，通过流加载预填充、分布式SLP、节点内预填充-解码分离、AVX-512优化的FP8 GEMV内核和细粒度CPU并行等方法，在消费级CPU-GPU平台上实现了云级服务质量，包括32K令牌30秒内完成预填充、解码吞吐量超过20 tokens/s等。

<h3 id="205">[205] MoE Enhanced Federated Learning for Spatiotemporal Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10499](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10499) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhehao Dai, Xiao Han, Zhaolin Deng, Zijian Zhang, Xiangyu Zhao, Guojiang Shen, Xiangjie Kong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Spatiotemporal Forecasting, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Summary**: 该论文针对跨城市时空预测中数据稀疏和隐私问题，提出MoE-FedTP，一种基于轻量级专家混合（MoE）网络的个性化联邦跨城市时空预测框架。通过部分参数共享从源城市导出专家网络，并使用门控机制动态融合以捕捉不同交通动态。在四个真实交通数据集上，MoE-FedTP优于最先进的跨城市和联邦学习基线。

<h3 id="206">[206] Assessing Automated Prompt Injection Attacks in Agentic Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10525](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10525) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David Hofer, Edoardo Debenedetti, Florian Tramèr
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Prompt Injection, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM智能体在不可信外部数据环境中的间接提示注入威胁，首次系统评估了自动化提示注入攻击在智能体环境中的有效性。改编了白盒（GCG）和黑盒（TAP）方法，在AgentDojo框架内跨越80个任务对、四个领域和多个模型进行评估。发现黑盒优化显著优于梯度方法，且任务通用攻击可跨领域迁移，但小型模型攻击无法迁移至GPT-5等前沿模型。

<h3 id="207">[207] Machine Learning Methods for Studying Latent Neural Activity Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10530](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10530) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shufeng Kong, Fumei Deng, Xinyi Dong, Caihua Liu, Weiwei Chen, Yingheng Wang, Daniel Cao, Azahara Oliva, Antonio Fernandez-Ruiz, Carla Gomes
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Computational Neuroscience, Representation Learning, Latent Variable Model
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文综述了从早期状态空间模型到最近深度生成模型在解码大规模神经元群体潜在结构方面的机器学习工具。将文献分为三个领域：单区域潜在动力学、多区域通信和行为对齐建模，并讨论了大规模神经基础模型、基准和开放挑战。

<h3 id="208">[208] LC-QAT: Data-Efficient 2-Bit QAT for LLMs via Linear-Constrained Vector Quantization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10531](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10531) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoyu Wang, Xingyu Yu, Haiyan Zhao, Fengxiang Wang, Xu Han
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantization, Model Compression, Quantization-Aware Training
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对极低比特大语言模型量化感知训练中，标量量化性能严重下降而向量量化无法端到端训练的问题，提出LC-QAT，一种2比特权重的向量量化感知训练框架。通过可学习的仿射映射表示量化权重，实现完全可微的端到端优化，无需显式码本查找。实验表明，LC-QAT仅需0.1%-10%的训练数据即可持续优于最先进的QAT方法。

<h3 id="209">[209] Flexible Flows for Biological Sequence Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10543](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10543) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yogesh Verma, Dani Korpela, Harri Lähdesmäki, Vikas Garg
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.ET; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 现有离散流匹配（DFM）方法在生物序列设计中受到非信息耦合和缺乏长度灵活性的限制。本文提出结构化耦合和基于潜在编辑的速率参数化，支持变长序列生成和细粒度控制。实验在密度估计、DNA生成和肽序列生成任务上达到最优性能。

<h3 id="210">[210] Benchmarking Knowledge Editing using Logical Rules</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10554](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10554) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tatiana Moteu Ngoli, NDah Jean Kouagou, Hamada M. Zahera, Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Editing, Benchmark
- **Venue**: 24th International Semantic Web Conference 2025
- **Summary**: 现有知识编辑基准主要评估直接事实回忆，忽略逻辑推论。本文提出基于知识图谱逻辑规则的多跳推理基准，衡量编辑后蕴含知识的影响。实验发现ROME和FT等方法在直接知识与蕴含知识间存在高达24%的性能差距。

<h3 id="211">[211] Hidden Consensus:Preference-Validity Compression in Human Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10569](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10569) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dorcas Chia Ern Chua, Karen Myn Hui Lee, Jia Yue Tan, Zhen Xue Gue, Norzalena Abdul Hamid, Azima Binti Azmi, Keat Mei Yeong, Aizat Izyani binti Mujab, Hafsah Noor Azam, Chee Guo Khoo, Han Ying Lim, Chee Seng Chan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RLHF, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: RLHF将多样的人类判断压缩为单一标量奖励，在多元社会中可能误测对齐。本文提出偏好有效性压缩概念，并以马来西亚为案例，发现79%的提示包含多个多数支持的回答，被丢弃的回答反映合理的文化框架。主张对齐方法应满足有效性保持的一致性。

<h3 id="212">[212] Improving Adversarial Transferability on Vision-Language Pre-training Models via Surrogate-Specific Bias Correction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10571](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10571) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lijia Yu, Jiuxin Cao, Yuchen Qiang, Changhao Chen, Yifei Huang, Bo Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 对抗攻击的跨模型迁移性受代理模型特定偏差限制。本文提出DeBias-Attack，通过主分支和参考分支的梯度投影修正偏差，并结合上下文感知文本替换。在多种VLP模型和下游任务上表现强劲。

<h3 id="213">[213] Convergence of Monte Carlo Optimistic Policy Iteration: Beyond Uniform State-Action Updates</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10580](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10580) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Octave Oliviers, Glenn Vinnicombe
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Policy Optimization
- **Summary**: Monte Carlo乐观策略迭代的收敛性此前需要均匀状态-行动更新，不切实际。本文证明仅需在每个状态下行动均匀更新即可保证收敛，允许状态以任意频率更新。通过均场动力学和锁定论证证明。

<h3 id="214">[214] Drawing with Strangers: Population Scaling Drives Zero-Shot Mutual Intelligibility in Emergent Sketching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10582](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10582) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jooyeon Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Emergent Communication, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 涌现通信中代理与陌生群组通信的零样本互理解能力研究不足。本文形式化零样本互理解（ZMI），发现扩大训练种群规模可显著提升ZMI，通过感知接地实现结构收敛。

<h3 id="215">[215] NOVA: Symbolic Regression Discovery of Interpretable Car-Following and Lane-Change Models with Driver Heterogeneity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10583](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10583) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ishak Abassi, Nassim Ali Bouazzouni, Farah Ibelaiden, Nadir Farhi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Symbolic Regression, Autonomous Driving
- **Summary**: 需要可解释的跟驰和换道模型。提出NOVA符号回归框架，从轨迹数据中自动发现紧凑加速度模型，RMSE=1.376 m/s²，在换道模型中达到67.4%平衡准确率，超越基线。

<h3 id="216">[216] Towards Diverse Scientific Hypothesis Search with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10587](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10587) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haorui Wang, Parshin Shojaee, Kazem Meidani, Kunyang Sun, José Miguel Hernández-Lobato, Teresa Head-Gordon, Jiajun He, Chandan K. Reddy, Chao Zhang, Yuanqi Du
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Discovery, Automated Research
- **Summary**: 假设搜索中的进化方法偏优化轻探索导致多样性崩溃。本文提出并行退火演化框架TempHyp，在不同温度水平搜索并交换信息。在分子发现、方程发现和算法发现任务上同时提升假设质量和多样性。

<h3 id="217">[217] From Data Heterogeneity to Convergence: A Data-Centric Review of Federated Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10595](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10595) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huong Nguyen, Mickaël Bettinelli, Amirhossein Ghaffari, Alexandre Benoit, Hong-Tri Nguyen, Susanna Pirttikangas, Lauri Lovén
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Survey
- **Summary**: 现有联邦学习综述缺乏数据视角的系统分析。本文从数据异质性度量、拆分协议和防御机制三方面，阐述数据属性对收敛速度和稳定性的影响，并提供可操作的指导。

<h3 id="218">[218] Embedding Hybrid Systems into Continuous Latent Vector Fields</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10596](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10596) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sangli Teng, Hang Liu, Koushil Sreenath
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Neural ODE, Dynamical Systems
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 混合系统的不连续性导致优化困难。本文证明n维混合系统可嵌入到m\>2n维空间中的连续向量场，并提出潜在神经ODE方法。实验表明该方法能从时间序列准确恢复混合系统流。

<h3 id="219">[219] Dmsh: A Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for All-Quad Mesh Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10601) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anirudh Kalyan, Cosmin Anitescu, Xiaoying Zhuang, Timon Rabczuk, Somdatta Goswami, Sundararajan Natarajan
- **Subjects**: math.NA; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Mesh Generation
- **Summary**: 高质量网格生成依赖启发式调参和半手动流程。提出Dmsh，首个全自动强化学习网格生成框架，通过三个协调代理处理拓扑简化、几何正则化和网格生成，使用参数化SAC。在多种基准上实现自动化、鲁棒性和质量提升。

<h3 id="220">[220] Causal Ensemble Agent: Hierarchical Causal Discovery with LLM-guided Expert Reweighting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10607) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Li, Yuanyuan Wang, Haoxuan Li, Chuan Zhou, Erdun Gao, Bo Han, Tongliang Liu, Kun Zhang, Howard Bondell, Mingming Gong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Causal Discovery, LLM-as-a-Judge
- **Summary**: 因果发现算法结果分歧，且忽略领域信息。提出因果集成代理（CEA），通过线性意见池整合统计专家，并用LLM作为元裁判动态重加权。在合成和真实数据集上取得最优因果图。

<h3 id="221">[221] Fast and Highly Expressive Policy Learning for Offline Reinforcement Learning via Bootstrapped Flow Q-Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10613) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thanh Nguyen, Tri Ton, Hongbin Choe, Tung M. Luu, Chang D. Yoo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Offline RL, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 扩散Q-learning因多步去噪计算昂贵。提出Bootstrapped Flow Q-Learning（BFQ），通过自举流匹配实现单步动作生成，无需辅助网络。在D4RL上提升性能同时显著降低计算成本。

<h3 id="222">[222] Can Image Models Imagine Time? ImageTime: A Novel Benchmark for Probing Visual World Modeling Through Spatiotemporal Consistency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10620](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10620) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinrui Wu, Lichen Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, Benchmark, Temporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 图像生成模型对时间变化的建模能力不明。提出ImageTime基准，使用四关键帧协议（初始、开始、过渡、结束）评估图像模型的时空一致性，通过VLM-as-judge打分。揭示了当前系统在维持时空连贯性上的成功与失败。

<h3 id="223">[223] STORM: Stepwise Token Optimization with Reward-Guided Beam Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10621](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10621) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arthur Satouf, Giulio D&#39;Erasmo, Yuxuan Zong, Habiboulaye Amadou Boubacar, Pablo Piantanida, Benjamin Piwowarski
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 词法检索器受词汇不匹配限制。提出STORM框架，通过检索奖励引导的逐步骤令牌优化和束搜索修剪训练重写器。在TREC DL和BEIR上匹配或超越先进方法，并零样本迁移到18种语言。

<h3 id="224">[224] Is Fairness Truly Fair? Towards Reliable Lipschitz Fairness in Multi-Task Learning via Fixed-\texorpdfstring{$δ$}{delta} Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10632) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junbo Ding, Xin Zang, Chenchen Pan, Donghao Song, Jiaxin Zhu, Danhuai Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Multi-Task Learning
- **Summary**: Lipschitz个体公平性评估在多任务学习中受表示尺度影响导致阈值混杂。提出ReLiF框架，使用固定δ审计和违规率反馈控制器。实验揭示固定阈值下真实的效用-公平权衡，并实现可控的公平正则化。

<h3 id="225">[225] Dynamic Linear Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10650](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10650) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xin Wang, Hui Shen, Boyuan Zheng, Xueshen Liu, Minkyoung Cho, Zhongwei Wan, Zesen Zhao, Zhuoqing Mao, Shen Yan, Mi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Linear Attention, Long Context
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有线性注意力方法使用固定状态合并策略，无法适应令牌重要性动态变化，导致长序列误差累积。提出动态线性注意力（DLA），包含信息感知动态状态合并和容量受限内存建模。在16个数据集上预训练和评估，表现优于现有方法。

<h3 id="226">[226] Post-Quantum Secure Federated DeFi for Inclusive Banking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10658) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Swati Sachan, Dale Fickett, Richard Buchinger, Theo Miller
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CE; q-fin.CP
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Post-Quantum Cryptography
- **Summary**: 量子计算威胁传统加密，DeFi系统面临安全风险，且金融服务对信用记录不足人群覆盖有限。提出后量子安全联邦DeFi框架，使用格基全同态加密和NASA-IBM地理空间基础模型，在加密下融合多银行数据。在弗吉尼亚州农业贷款场景中测试验证。

<h3 id="227">[227] Accounting for AI Inference in Corporate GHG Inventories: A Four-Tier Methodology for Scope 3 Category 1 Reporting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10660](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10660) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guillermo Llopis
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Sustainability, Carbon Accounting
- **Summary**: 企业温室气体清单缺乏AI推理排放核算标准，现有方法高估10-40倍。提出四层框架，从直接令牌估算到支出分摊法，基于GPU能耗基准和区域碳强度。应用于200人公司，总排放低于1 tCO2e，并揭示水-碳权衡。

<h3 id="228">[228] Decentralized Multi-Agent Systems with Shared Context</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10662](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10662) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuzhen Mao, Azalia Mirhoseini
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Decentralized Computing
- **Summary**: 集中式多智能体系统存在通信瓶颈。提出去中心化语言模型（DeLM），通过并行智能体、共享验证上下文和任务队列实现异步协作。在SWE-bench Verified上性能提升达10.5个百分点，成本降低约50%；在LongBench-v2上提升5.7个百分点。

<h3 id="229">[229] In Defense of Information Leakage in Concept-based Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10669](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10669) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Concept-based Models
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 概念模型传统观点认为信息泄漏应被消除，但实际中概念不完整时泄漏对准确性和可干预性必要。提出良性泄漏概念，通过重构训练目标让模型利用泄漏而不牺牲性能。

<h3 id="230">[230] UniDexTok: A Unified Dexterous Hand Tokenizer from Real Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10683](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10683) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dong Fang, Youjun Wu, Yuanxin Zhong, Rui Zhang, Yunlong Wang, Xiaosong Jia, Yu-Gang Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robotics, Dexterous Manipulation, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 灵巧手结构差异导致数据碎片化，难以联合训练。提出统一灵巧手模型（UDHM）和免重定向状态分词器UniDexTok，将不同构型映射到22自由度语义接口。误差减少98%以上，跨构体重建精度达亚毫米级。

<h3 id="231">[231] Divide and Cooperate: Role-Decomposed Multi-Agent LLM Training with Cross-Agent Learning Signals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10684) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jaewan Park, Solbee Cho, Jay-Yoon Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 单策略耦合证据获取与答案生成，导致信用分配问题。提出角色分解多智能体训练框架DAC，分离搜索和生成角色，利用弃权信号提供跨智能体学习信号。在QA基准上仅用LoRA即达到强性能。

<h3 id="232">[232] Using the YOLOv12 Model for Verifying the Correct Color Sequence of Wires in Network Cables (Patch Cords) on the Production Line</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10699](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10699) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Amin Doroodchi, Danial Soleimany
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Manufacturing AI
- **Summary**: 网线线序人工检测耗时易错。提出基于YOLOv12的智能检测系统。精度约98%，平均准确率约95%，召回率约98%，可实时自动验证线序。

<h3 id="233">[233] Event-Driven Reinforcement Learning Enables Long-Horizon Control in Semiconductor Fabrication</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10705](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10705) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yavar Yeganeh, Mahsa Shekari, Nicla Frigerio, Daniele Pagano, Andrea Matta
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Event-Driven Control, Manufacturing AI
- **Summary**: 半导体制造系统复杂、高维、延迟反馈，生产规划困难。提出深度强化学习框架，将控制问题建模为集中式智能体，采用事件驱动时间差分方法。在高保真仿真中显著提升吞吐量和利用率。

<h3 id="234">[234] Unifying Data, Memory, and Compute Efficiency in LLM training: A Survey</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10706](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10706) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vanessa Schmidt, Huy Hoang Nguyen, Cédric Jung, Shirin Salehi, Anke Schmeink
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Data Selection, Memory Efficiency
- **Summary**: LLM效率研究常孤立技术，缺乏系统视角。本综述从数据效率、内存效率和计算预算意识三个耦合瓶颈组织进展。强调最优数据子集依赖于任务目标和资源预算，且GPU内存是微调主要瓶颈。

<h3 id="235">[235] Effective Reinforcement Learning for Agentic Search by Recycling Zero-Variance Queries During Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10709](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10709) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: João Coelho, João Magalhães, Bruno Martins, Chenyan Xiong
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Query Recycling
- **Summary**: GRPO风格算法中零方差查询组（全对/全错）浪费rollout成本。提出查询回收机制，将零方差组放回可变池重新采样。1.7B模型在7个多跳QA基准上平均Pass@1达66.0，匹配7B模型。

<h3 id="236">[236] ++nnU-Net: Scaling nnU-Net with Prefix-Based Data Augmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10713](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10713) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ana Sofia Santos, André Ferreira, Gijs Luijten, Naida Solak, Lisle Faray de Paiva, Behrus Hinrichs-Puladi, Jens Kleesiek, Jan Egger, Victor Alves
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation, Data Augmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sofia-adelie/plusplusnnunet.git)
- **Summary**: 医学影像数据采集受隐私和标注成本限制，数据增强需保持解剖可行性。提出++nnU-Net，在预处理前使用图像配准增强。在5个2D数据集上，Dice系数最高提升约22%。

<h3 id="237">[237] Attention Expansion: Enhancing Keyphrase Extraction from Long Documents with Attention-Augmented Contextualized Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10716](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10716) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Roberto Martínez-Cruz, Alvaro J. López-López, José Portela
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Keyphrase Extraction, Long Context, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 长文档关键短语抽取中，重要证据分散，PLM上下文窗口有限。提出注意力扩展机制，利用词嵌入补充超上下文信息。在5个基准上显著提升F1，优于现有模型。

<h3 id="238">[238] Transformer Based Model for Spatiotemporal Feature Learning in EEG Emotion Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10718) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinglong Cui, Dian Gu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: EEG Classification, Emotion Recognition, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: EEG情感识别中，现有模型难以充分捕获时空特征。提出EEG-TransNet，包含局部自注意力和模糊注意力同步变换器。在三个数据集上分类准确率和鲁棒性优于其他方法。

<h3 id="239">[239] Detecting Knowledge Gaps from Conversational AI Interactions Using Curriculum Prerequisite Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10736](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10736) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youssef Medhat, Junsoo Park, Ploy Thajchayapong, Ashok K. Goel
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Knowledge Graph
- **Venue**: 10th CSEDM Workshop
- **Summary**: 在线课程中学生与AI助教的交互日志未被用于诊断知识缺口。提出流水线，将学生问题映射到课程主题，利用GPT-4提取先修知识图。在1340个问题上准确率80.0%，问题数量与自报难度显著相关。

<h3 id="240">[240] Spatial-Omni: Spatial Audio Understanding Integration in Multimodal LLMs via FOA Encoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10738) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiyuan Zhu, Yixuan Chen, Yiwen Shao, Wenxiang Guo, Changhao Pan, Yu Zhang, Yuxiang Wang, Wei Liu, Houhua Zhang, Chengkuan Zeng, Wenbo Cheng, Yunxi Liu, Rui Yang, Steve Yves, Liefeng Bo, Zhou Zhao
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Audio-Language Model, Spatial Audio
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dieKarotte/Spatial-Omni)
- **Summary**: 多模态大语言模型将音频处理为单声道，丢失空间信息。提出Spatial-Omni，通过SO-Encoder将一阶Ambisonics空间音频作为独立模态注入，无需修改原音频编码器。在空间音频理解任务上超越现有模型，且保持通用音频能力。

<h3 id="241">[241] Toward Secure LLM Agents: Threat Surfaces, Attacks, Defenses, and Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10749](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10749) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuchen Ling, Shengcheng Yu, Zhenyu Chen, Chunrong Fang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Prompt Injection, Model Security
- **Summary**: 针对LLM Agent面临的安全威胁（如提示注入、控制流劫持、状态破坏等），本文通过生命周期框架综述了247篇论文，分析了威胁面、攻击家族和防御措施。研究发现提示注入和工具中介劫持仍占主导，持久状态破坏和多Agent传播成为新兴关注点；现有防御方法组合性弱，基准测试不足。强调需要明确的信任边界、权限控制和状态管理。

<h3 id="242">[242] A Bayesian Network Approach for Enhancing Security-Focused Decision Support Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10782](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10782) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Carolina Fernández-Martínez, Shuaib Siddiqui, Vanesa Daza
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Bayesian Network, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 针对异构网络环境中安全工具选择困难的问题，本文提出基于贝叶斯网络的决策支持系统（DSS），通过捕获用户对安全三要素的高层需求，推理出最优工具。系统在时间和预测准确率上进行了性能评估。

<h3 id="243">[243] Dep-LLM: Training-Free Depression Diagnosis via Evidence-Guided Structured Multi-factor with Reliable LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10796](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10796) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiqing Lyu, Xianbing Zhao, Buzhou Tang, Ronghuan Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Clinical NLP, Mental Health
- **Summary**: 针对临床访谈中抑郁症自动检测的困难（稀疏线索、标注数据少），本文提出无需训练的Dep-LLM框架，通过结构化多因素分析、置信度调节和协作预测实现诊断。在DAIC-WOZ和E-DAIC数据集上超越现有监督模型和闭源LLM。

<h3 id="244">[244] Boosting ECG Classification Performance by Pre-training with Synthesized Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10802) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Naoki Nonaka, Jun Seita
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: ECG Analysis, Synthetic Data Generation, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 针对医疗ECG分类中数据稀缺问题，本文采用基于领域知识的高斯组合合成算法生成合成ECG数据，并通过预训练提升分类性能。在四种异常类别中，AFLT性能平均提升33.2%，小真实数据集上收益更大。

<h3 id="245">[245] Beyond APIs: Probing the Limits of MLLMs in Physical Tool Use</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10803](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10803) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhixin Ma, Yutong Zhou, Yongqi Li, Chong-Wah Ngo, Wenjie Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Multimodal LLM, Embodied AI, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 本文引入PhysTool-Bench基准测试，评估多模态大语言模型在物理工具使用中的感知和规划能力。最好的模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅识别58.7%的工具，端到端完成21.0%的查询，暴露出感知和功能常识的严重缺陷。

<h3 id="246">[246] Earth-OneVision: Extending Remote Sensing Multimodal Large Language Models to More Sensor Modalities and Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10819](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10819) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Miaoxin Cai, Guanqun Wang, Wei Zhang, Guangyao Zhou, Yin Zhuang, Tong Zhang, Hao Wang, He Chen, Jun Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Multimodal LLM, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对现有遥感多模态大语言模型支持的传感器类型和任务有限的问题，本文提出Earth-OneVision，统一六种传感器模态和九类任务。通过全粒度对齐、序列化和渐进跨模态适应机制，在多个基准上达到最优，超越更大参数模型。

<h3 id="247">[247] K-Forcing: Joint Next-K-Token Decoding via Push-Forward Language Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10820](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10820) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiwei Tang, Yuanyu He, Yizheng Han, Wangbo Zhao, Jiasheng Tang, Fan Wang, Bohan Zhuang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Parallel Decoding, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对自回归解码的内存瓶颈和高负载批处理效率低的问题，本文提出K-Forcing，通过推前语言模型一次生成k个token，并采用渐进自强制蒸馏训练。在LM1B和OpenWebText上实现2.4-3.5倍加速，质量损失较小。

<h3 id="248">[248] A Unified Siamese Learning Framework for Zero-Day Anomaly Detection and Classification in Optical Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10827](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10827) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Carlos Natalino, Flávia P. Monteiro, Paolo Monti
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Few-Shot Learning, Optical Networks
- **Venue**: Optical Fiber Communication Conference (OFC) 2026
- **Summary**: 针对光网络中的零日异常检测和分类问题，本文提出多相似度孪生神经网络，实现99%以上准确率，无需重训练即可适应新光路和未知异常类型。

<h3 id="249">[249] Attention-Discounted Adaptive Sampler for Masked Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10829](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10829) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yusuf Sahin, Ahmed Rockey Saikia, Volkan Cevher, Paolo Favaro
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Language Model, Parallel Decoding, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对掩码扩散语言模型中并行解码时标记耦合导致的生成不稳定问题，本文提出ADAS，一种训练自由的重排序方法，通过注意力折扣降低与不确定已选标记强相关的候选。在多个基准上低NFE性能提升约10个百分点，开销极低。

<h3 id="250">[250] Geometrically Averaged Hard Target Updates for Linear Q-Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10835](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10835) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Donghwan Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Deep Q-Learning
- **Summary**: 本文引入λ-目标更新，对线性Q学习的周期性目标更新进行几何加权平均，并利用切换系统模型分析其稳定性。理论分析了确定性子版本的性质。

<h3 id="251">[251] Janus: A Benchmark for Goal-Conditioned Information Distortion in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10852](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10852) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Polydoros Giannouris, Mohsinul Kabir, Sophia Ananiadou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Misinformation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM输出中有选择性地呈现事实导致误导性通信的问题，本文提出JANUS基准，测量目标条件下的信息失真。实验显示12种LLM均存在一致失真，缺乏对选择性误导的防护。

<h3 id="252">[252] From Perception to Action: Can UI Interventions Foster Sustainable LLM Chatbot</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10861) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nitish Patkar, Pooja Rani, Jack Glässer, Simon Lüscher, Martin Kropp
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: AI Sustainability, Human-Computer Interaction, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 本文探索UI干预对LLM聊天机器人能源意识的影响，通过原型现场研究发现节能模式使用率为55.8%，大多数参与者在非高精度需求时主动选择节能模式。表明可持续性UI干预可改善能源意识。

<h3 id="253">[253] LIBERO-Occ: Evaluating and Improving Vision-Language-Action Models under Scene-Induced Occlusion via Viewpoint Imagination</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10862](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10862) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Taishan Li, Jiwen Zhang, Siyuan Wang, Xuanjing Huang, Zhongyu Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Robot Manipulation, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/litsh/Libero-Occ)
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-动作模型在遮挡下性能下降的问题，本文提出LIBERO-Occ基准和视角想象力（VIM）方法，通过生成互补视图提升鲁棒性，无需额外相机。实验表明VIM有效改善遮挡下的操作性能。

<h3 id="254">[254] Optimal Post-Training Quantization Scales and Where to Find Them</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10890](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10890) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juan Amboage, Pablo Monteagudo-Lago, Ian Colbert, Giuseppe Franco, Nicholas Fraser
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Quantization, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对后训练量化中缩放因子选择问题，本文提出PiSO算法，利用校准数据在四舍五入量化下精确求解最优通道尺度，并扩展到分组量化。在Llama和Qwen模型上，困惑度和准确率均有提升，低位宽效果更显著。

<h3 id="255">[255] Improving Text-Instance Alignment Of Foreground Conditioned Out-Painting Via Customized Concept Embedding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10892](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10892) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yihao Zhao, Xuan Han, Bin He, Mingyu You
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Diffusion Model, Image Editing
- **Summary**: 针对前景条件外绘画中的伪影问题（合成背景与前景语义重叠），本文提出CCE-Diffusion框架，通过定制概念嵌入模块和改进损失函数减少伪影，可即插即用于多种外绘画方法。

<h3 id="256">[256] Pose-ICL: 3D-Aware In-Context Learning for Pose-Controllable Subject Customization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10902](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10902) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuan Han, Yihao Zhao, Mingyu You
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Image Generation, 3D Vision
- **Summary**: 针对主体定制中姿态控制困难（姿态不准确、身份不一致），本文提出Pose-ICL，一种无需微调的3D感知上下文学习框架，通过表面锚定位置编码实现显式3D意识，在姿态准确性和身份一致性上超越现有方法。

<h3 id="257">[257] Human-AI Teaming Through the Lens of Calibration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10906](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10906) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric Nalisnick, Chi Zhang, Sophia Qian, Yixin Wang
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Human-AI Interaction, Model Calibration, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 该论文探讨了统计校准在人类-AI团队协作中的传播效应。通过理论和实证分析，研究了组合与委派两种框架中校准假设的影响。发现组合方法无法保持人类的校准度，而委派方法虽保留校准但要求拒绝器模型具有足够精细的校准能力，在人类依赖系统不可观测信息时难以实现。

<h3 id="258">[258] RAT: Reference-Augmented Training for ASV Anti-Spoofing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10908](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10908) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vojtěch Staněk, Anton Firc, Jakub Reš, Kamil Malinka
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Speech Processing
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 针对ASV反欺骗任务，论文提出参考增强训练策略（RAT），在训练时使用说话人参考通道但推理时忽略。该方法使模型在推理时即使参考被替换为零向量仍能提升检测性能。在ASVspoof 5上实现2.57%等错误率和0.074 minDCF，达到当前最优。

<h3 id="259">[259] Ethical and Technical Limits of Deepfake Speech Datasets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10911](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10911) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vojtěch Staněk, Eva Trnovská, Kamil Malinka, Anton Firc
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Dataset, Fairness
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文对39个深度伪造语音数据集进行了审计，发现大多数数据集缺少人口统计元数据，且底层真实语音源语料存在大量重叠。这导致公平性评估难以进行，跨数据集评估可能夸大泛化性能。

<h3 id="260">[260] What Do Deepfake Speech Detectors Actually Hear?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10912](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10912) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vojtěch Staněk, Veronika Jirmusová, Anton Firc, Kamil Malinka, Jakub Reš, Martin Perešíni
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Deepfake Detection, Interpretability
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 针对深度伪造语音检测器缺乏可解释性的问题，提出基于集成梯度的音频解释管线，在时间对齐的自监督表示上定位决策证据。发现不同检测器依赖不同线索（如非语音/环境、音素伪影、词边界），并通过因果掩码验证。

<h3 id="261">[261] A Constrained Natural-Language Interface for Variational Multi-Physics Finite Element Simulations in FEniCS</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10928](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10928) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nilay Upadhyay, Wesley F. Reinhart
- **Subjects**: cs.CE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Application, Scientific Computing, Physics Simulation
- **Summary**: 为解决LLM生成有限元求解器代码的可靠性问题，提出约束自然语言接口，限制LLM仅处理前端任务（解析、几何代码），而核心求解器使用人工编写的模板。在解析测试中实现100%有效解析率，在自定义几何基准中达到90%成功率，并成功生成了3D弹塑性L形支架的仿真。

<h3 id="262">[262] Recoverable but Not Stationary:Local Linear Structures in Weights and Activations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10929](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10929) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Irina Piontkovskaia, Sergey Nikolenko
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Representation Learning, Training Dynamics
- **Summary**: 该论文研究训练网络中线性结构的本质，发现局部低秩任务梯度结构显著，但固定任务平面假设不成立。在LoRA恢复中，轨迹前缀基捕获了77%的位移。同时验证了参数扰动与激活操控之间的余弦相似性约为0.58。

<h3 id="263">[263] CLP: Collocation-Length Prediction for Zero-Loss Adaptive Multi-Token Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10935](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10935) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuezhen Xie, Zhiqiang Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Speculative Decoding, Inference Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对多令牌预测加速LLM推理时质量下降的问题，提出主干即架构原则和搭配长度预测器（CLP）。CLP仅需单线性层，在1.5B和7B模型上分别实现1.20倍和1.14倍加速，且零质量损失，而基于门控的方法失败。

<h3 id="264">[264] Provenance Tracking in AI Compilers through the Lens of Coalgebra</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10937](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10937) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zilu Tian, Liying Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Provenance, Program Analysis, Compiler Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对AI编译器重写计算图导致溯源困难的问题，提出基于观测语义的生成式溯源方法，利用余代数模型和互模拟。在原型编译器COVAN中实现，可在编译流水线中稳定追踪溯源，工程开销极低。

<h3 id="265">[265] Democratising Camera Trap AI: An Open-Source Model for Detecting UK Mammals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10940) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Paul Fergus, Philip Stephens, Russell A. Hill, Lee Oliver, Katie Appleby, Sarah Beatham, Naomi Davies Walsh, Stuart Nixon, Naomi Matthews, Chris Sutherland, Kelly Hitchcock
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Animal Recognition, Ecological AI
- **Summary**: 为降低相机陷阱AI的使用门槛，该研究开源了一个基于YOLO26x的物体检测模型，可识别31个类别（包括28种英国常见哺乳动物和鸟类）。在验证集上mAP达到0.984，精度0.988，召回率0.965，假阴性率仅0.17%。

<h3 id="266">[266] Generative Explainability for Next-Generation Networks: LLM-Augmented XAI with Mutual Feature Interactions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10942](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10942) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kiarash Rezaei, Omran Ayoub, Sebastian Troia, Francesco Lelli, Paolo Monti, Carlos Natalino
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Network AI, LLM Application
- **Venue**: 2025 21th International Conference on Wireless and Mobile Computing, Networking and Communications (WiMob)
- **Summary**: 针对网络运营中AI模型缺乏可解释性的问题，提出结合LLM和互特征交互增强的XAI框架。该框架通过结构化提示词整合SHAP特征值和交互信息，生成自然语言解释。在光网络QoT估计用例中，解释有用性和范围分别提升12.2%和6.2%，正确率97.5%。

<h3 id="267">[267] Beyond Uniform Token-Level Trust Region in LLM Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10968](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10968) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Renjie Mao, Xiangxin Zhou, Lvfang Tao, Yixin Ding, Yu Shi, Yongguang Lin, Yuheng Wu, Honglin Zhu, Qian Qiu, Wenxi Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning, Policy Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对RLVR中PPO类信任区域机制忽略自回归不对称性和累计前缀偏移的问题，提出累计前缀差异策略优化（CPPO）。通过位置加权阈值和累计前缀预算，提升了训练稳定性和推理准确性。

<h3 id="268">[268] Optimizing 2D Input Representations and Sub-phase Fusion Strategies for Differential Diagnosis of Asthma and COPD Using CNN- and GRU-Based Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10972](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10972) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ipek Sen, Ozgur Ozdemir, Elena Battini Sonmez
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Audio Processing, Deep Learning
- **Summary**: 该研究探索了不同2D输入表示和子阶段融合策略在哮喘与COPD鉴别诊断中的应用。采用自适应长度窗口和MFCC、对数梅尔谱等特征，结合CNN/GRU融合。最佳周期级F1为0.877（MFCC+直接拼接），数据增强反而降低了性能。

<h3 id="269">[269] Understanding and mitigating the risks of OpenClaw for non-technical users: A practical guide with Skill</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11007) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junchang Zheng, Junfeng Tan, Jialiang Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, Human-AI Interaction
- **Summary**: 针对非技术用户使用OpenClaw智能体框架的风险，该文识别了七类核心风险并提供了易懂的防御策略，同时开发了自动化安全配置的Skill。旨在让非技术用户也能安全使用智能体框架。

<h3 id="270">[270] Diffusion Forcing Planner: History-Annealed Planning with Time-Dependent Guidance for Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11019) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zehan Zhang, Neng Zhang, Yaoyi Li, Jia Cai, Zhiling Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Diffusion Model, Path Planning
- **Summary**: 针对学习型运动规划器的时间不一致性，提出扩散强制规划器（DFP），通过异质联合扩散过程和基于退火历史的无分类器引导。在nuPlan闭环评估中实现了竞争性性能，生成连续、稳定且可控的运动轨迹。

<h3 id="271">[271] AuRA: Internalizing Audio Understanding into LLMs as LoRA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11033](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11033) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bo Cheng, Lei Shi, Zhanyu Ma, Yuan Wu, Jun Xu, Jiuchong Gao, Jinghua Hao, Renqing He
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Knowledge Distillation, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 为克服级联ASR-LLM和端到端语音语言模型的局限，提出AuRA，将音频编码能力蒸馏到LLM的LoRA适配器中。通过层间蒸馏对齐学生和教师表示，在多个语音语言基准上优于级联系统和桥接方法。

<h3 id="272">[272] T1-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-Scenario Agents in Real-World Domains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11070](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11070) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Genta Indra Winata, Amartya Chakraborty, Yuzhen Lin, Swasthi P Rao, Shikhhar Siingh, Houhan Lu, Nadia Bathaee, Sriharsha Hatwar, Paresh Dashore, Anmol Jain, Kshitij Tayal, Xiuzhu Lin, Anirban Das, Sambit Sahu, Shi-Xiong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对现有智能体基准在任务复杂性和领域多样性上的不足，提出T1-Bench，覆盖25个领域的多场景交互式基准。在12个模型上进行了评估，并结合人工判断，为多步骤、跨域智能体评估提供了标准化框架。

<h3 id="273">[273] Modeling Complex Behaviors: Multi-Personality Composition and Dynamic Switching in Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11074](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11074) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peiqi Jia, Haonan Jia, Ziqi Miao, Linkang Du, Yuntao Wang, Zhou Su
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Persona, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 现有多模态大语言模型(MLLM)缺乏对复杂人格条件的理解和控制，提示词方法在多模态场景下泛化有限。本文提出显式人格条件建模，并建立了包含单人格诱导、多人格诱导和人格切换的系统评估框架。实验表明人格诱导提升图像描述性能但损害视觉问答性能，多人格组合和动态切换中存在平衡和残留效应。

<h3 id="274">[274] Unifying Local Communications and Local Updates for LLM Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11081](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11081) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pietro Cagnasso, Eugene Belilovsky, Edouard Oyallon
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Decentralized Learning, Gossip Protocol
- **Summary**: 分布式LLM预训练中同步All-Reduce通信成为瓶颈，尤其在异构带宽环境下。本文提出GASLoC算法，一种基于八卦通信的分散式预训练算法，兼容自适应优化器和本地步骤。实验表明GASLoC在单步通信设置下优于现有分散算法，且在异构带宽下显著优于DiLoCo。

<h3 id="275">[275] Test-Time Gradient Guidance of Flow Policies in Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11087](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11087) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiyuan Zhou, Andy Peng, Charles Xu, Qiyang Li, Tobias Springenberg, Kevin Frans, Sergey Levine
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Flow Matching, Imitation Learning
- **Summary**: 将扩散/流策略纳入RL管道存在稳定性问题，需要特殊训练目标或反向传播。本文提出QGF算法，在测试时使用值函数梯度引导参考流策略生成高价值动作，无需额外策略学习。实验表明QGF在离线RL基准上优于测试时方法，与训练时算法竞争力强且成本更低。

<h3 id="276">[276] RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11092](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11092) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yichao Zhong, Yidan Lu, Yuhang Lu, Tianyang Tang, Haoguang Mai, Yixuan Pan, Tianyu Li, Li Chen, Jingbo Wang, Zhongyu Li, Peng Lu, Hongyang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Humanoid Robot, Curriculum Learning
- **Summary**: 类人机器人足球射门需要全身稳定性、高冲力和准确性，现有方法难以适应不同球位置和击球时机。本文提出RoboNaldo，一个三阶段运动引导课程RL框架，逐步从稳定踢腿先验过渡到自由球和移动球射门。模拟中自由球误差降低48.6%，速度提升2.96倍；真实机器人上从3米处射门平均误差0.73米，球速达13.10米/秒。

<h3 id="277">[277] PhantomBench: Benchmarking the Non-existential Threat of Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11105](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11105) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haeji Jung, Hila Gonen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 语言模型对不存在概念产生幻觉，但缺乏评估其知识边界识别能力的基准。本文构建PhantomBench，包含6万多个虚假概念，评估21个模型。结果显示平均幻觉率高达86.7%，前沿模型在输入假定存在时也失败；该基准可作为研究稀有概念行为的代理。

<h3 id="278">[278] FADA: Accessible fetal ultrasound interpretation and annotation with a selectively distilled unified vision-language model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11106](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11106) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mahmood Alzubaidi, Uzair Shah, Raden Muaz, Ines Abbes, Nader Mohammed, Abdullatif Magram, Khalid Alyafei, Mowafa Househ, Marco Agus
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Knowledge Distillation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mahmoodphd/FADA)
- **Summary**: 低中收入国家缺乏训练有素的超声技师，现有深度学习模型孤立处理检测、分割或分类。本文提出FADA统一视觉语言模型，仅通过解释优先流程执行临床解释、分类、检测和分割。选择性蒸馏策略，分割Dice 0.8820，检测mAP 0.7671，解释合规100%；可在智能手机上离线运行，全程约60秒。

<h3 id="279">[279] Designed by Journalists, but Is It for Readers? Rethinking AI Disclosures and Transparency in News</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11116) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pooja Prajod
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, AI Ethics, Trust Calibration
- **Venue**: CHIWORK Workshop
- **Summary**: 新闻中AI披露未能建立读者信任，详细披露反而降低信任。本文通过34名读者的对照实验发现详细披露引发透明度困境，单行披露造成信息鸿沟。读者偏好用户代理设计（按需详情、AI比例可视化等），揭示实践者与用户需求之间的脱节。

<h3 id="280">[280] Towards Autonomous Accelerator Design: FPGA Accelerator Generation with SECDA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11117](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11117) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vinamra Sharma, Xingjian Fu, Jude Haris, José Cano
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: FPGA, Design Space Exploration, LLM for EDA
- **Venue**: Machine Learning for Architecture and Systems Workshop (MLArchSys)
- **Summary**: 基于FPGA的AI加速器设计空间探索耗时且依赖领域知识。本文提出SECDA-DSE框架，集成LLM通过RAG和思维链引导探索，并结合反馈循环。生成了三个加速器设计（向量乘法、2D卷积、矩阵转置），成功在FPGA上运行，展示了LLM引导探索的潜力。

<h3 id="281">[281] TRACE: A Unified Rollout Budget Allocation Framework for Efficient Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11119) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Heming Zou, Qi Wang, Yun Qu, Yuhang Jiang, Lizhou Cai, Yixiu Mao, Ru Peng, Xin Xu, Weijie Liu, Kai Yang, Saiyong Yang, Xiangyang Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RLVR, LLM Agent, Multi-Turn Interaction
- **Summary**: 基于可验证奖励的RL中， rollout预算分配常忽略前缀级信息，导致奖励对比不足。本文提出TRACE框架，将预算分配给提示根和中间前缀，形成树形rollout。在Multi-Hop QA基准上，Qwen3-14B准确率提升2.8个点。

<h3 id="282">[282] Provenance-Grounded Gating and Adaptive Recovery in Synthetic Post-Training Data Curation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11127](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11127) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soham Bhattacharjee, Karun Sharma, Vinay Kumar Sankarapu, Pratinav Seth
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Synthetic Data Generation, LLM Training, Data Quality
- **Summary**: 合成后训练数据过滤常缺乏源证据依据，且拒绝样本被永久丢弃。本文系统研究了基于来源的门控和自适应恢复流程。实验表明精确来源对强判别器有益，幻觉门和奖励门互补，自适应恢复管道在产量、恢复率和注入召回上优于简单重采样。

<h3 id="283">[283] Data assimilation for subsurface flow using latent diffusion model parameterization: performance of ensemble-Kalman and Monte Carlo techniques</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11140](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11140) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guido Di Federico, Wenchao Teng, Louis J. Durlofsky
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.AP; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Surrogate Model, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 潜扩散模型参数化在地下水数据同化中可能降低卡尔曼类方法的性能。本文比较了ESMDA、MCMC和SMC算法在高维3D通道化地质模型上的表现。结果表明潜空间更新保持地质真实性但不确定性降低有限，而MCMC和SMC在数据匹配和不确定性降低上更优。

<h3 id="284">[284] Flaws in the LLM Automation Narrative</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11166](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11166) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: George Perrett, Javae Elliott, Jennifer Hill, Marc Scott
- **Subjects**: stat.OT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Code Generation
- **Summary**: LLM基准测试常忽略性能方差和错误幅度，导致自动化叙事夸大。本文通过一个数据分析编程任务比较前沿LLM与人类专家，并显式测量方差和错误幅度。结果表明人类专家在多个指标上平均表现更好且变异性更小，LLM未达到人类专家水平。

<h3 id="285">[285] Piper: A Programmable Distributed Training System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11169](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11169) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Megan Frisella, Shubham Tiwari, Andy Ruan, Yi Pan, Parker Gustafson, Mat Jacob, Gilbert Bernstein, Stephanie Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Distributed Training, LLM Training, System Design
- **Summary**: 大规模模型训练依赖人工设计并行策略，系统难以适应新策略。本文提出Piper，将策略与运行时解耦，用户通过声明式指令定义策略。Piper将训练图编译为设备执行计划，实验表明在常见策略上保持性能，并可在组合策略（如DeepSeek-V3的DualPipe）中实现额外效率提升。

<h3 id="286">[286] EEVEE: Towards Test-time Prompt Learning in the Real World for Self-Improving Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11182](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11182) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weixian Xu, Shilong Liu, Mengdi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Test-Time Adaptation, Prompt Engineering, Self-Improvement
- **Summary**: 现有测试时提示学习方法针对单数据集设计，无法处理真实世界的异构数据流。本文提出EEVEE，首个多数据集测试时提示学习框架，包含路由器和路由器-提示协同进化策略。实验表明多基准平均分提升10.38和24.32点，超越SOTA方法37.2%和48.2%。

<h3 id="287">[287] A Unifying Lens on Supervised Fine-Tuning Through Target Distribution Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11189) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tong Xie, Yuanhao Ban, Yunqi Hong, Sohyun An, Yihang Chen, Cho-Jui Hsieh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Fine-Tuning, Instruction Tuning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 监督微调(SFT)通常最大化每个token的似然，但可能次优。本文提出Q-target框架，将SFT重新解释为目标分布设计，并据此提出Target-SFT方法。在十个推理数据集-模型设置上，Target-SFT一致优于基线。

<h3 id="291">[291] Position: The ML Community Must Build an AI-Augmented Peer-Review Ecosystem</h3>

<h3 id="292">[292] Why Does Reasoning Length Converge? Unveiling the Underfitting-Overfitting Trade-off in Chain-of-Thought</h3>

<h3 id="293">[293] Constructing coherent spatial memory in LLM agents through graph rectification</h3>

<h3 id="296">[296] How can we assess human-agent interactions? Case studies in software agent design</h3>

<h3 id="297">[297] RoboGPT-R1: Enhancing Robot Task Planning with Reinforcement Learning</h3>

<h3 id="299">[299] The Price of Agreement: Measuring LLM Sycophancy in Agentic Financial Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.24668](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24668) (replaced)

<h3 id="300">[300] Adaptive Teacher Exposure for Self-Distillation in LLM Reasoning</h3>

<h3 id="301">[301] CatalyticMLLM: A Graph-Text Multimodal Large Language Model for Catalytic Materials</h3>

<h3 id="302">[302] Neurosymbolic Learning for Inference-Time Argumentation</h3>

<h3 id="303">[303] AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments</h3>

<h3 id="304">[304] A Sober Look at Agentic Misalignment in Automated Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24197](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24197) (replaced)

<h3 id="305">[305] EVA-Net: Subject-Independent EEG Motor Decoding with Video-Derived Motor Priors</h3>

<h3 id="306">[306] VET: A Framework for Analyzing AI Discourse</h3>

<h3 id="309">[309] PSEBench: A Controllable and Verifiable Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs in Patient Safety Event Triage</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, LLM Hallucination
- **Summary**: 针对医疗大模型需持续护理而非单轮问答的问题，构建Baichuan-M4临床级医疗智能体系统，集成统一运行时、连续护理强化学习框架（含跨度级奖励建模、课程学习等）及临床工具层。在多项医学评估上取得领先结果，幻觉率降至3.3%。

<h3 id="312">[312] Graph2Idea:Retrieval-Augmented Scientific Idea Generation with Graph-Structured Contexts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09105](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09105) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xu Li, Hanzhe Tu, Xun Han
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Scientific Reasoning, Graph Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM科研想法生成中平坦文本上下文缺乏跨论文关系的问题，提出Graph2Idea框架，将检索论文转化为知识图谱并提取图结构上下文，通过两阶段生成识别研究方向并合成候选想法。在自动评估中新颖性、质量和可行性分别达到0.52、0.29和0.28，优于基线。

<h3 id="313">[313] Anything2Skill: Compiling External Knowledge into Reusable Skills for Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09316](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09316) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qianjun Pan, Yutao Yang, Junsong Li, Jie Zhou, Kai Chen, Xin Li, Qin Chen, Liang He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Agent, Skill Learning
- **Summary**: 针对RAG仅检索碎片化证据而智能体需反复推断过程的问题，提出Anything2Skill框架，从异构知识中提取可重用技能并存入SkillBank，推理时同时检索证据和技能。在qsv和GitHub-CLI任务上成功率分别达98.85%和94.10%，远超纯RAG智能体。

<h3 id="314">[314] Emergent alignment and the projectability of ethical personas</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09475) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guillermo Del Pinal, Youngchan Lee, Calum McNamara, Alejandro Perez Carballo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, AI Ethics, Safety Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对“突现失调”现象，研究其反面“突现对齐”，通过用不同宪法（功利主义、义务论等）微调模型，发现窄安全任务微调可诱导广泛的突现对齐。模型获得预期伦理人格，但投射程度存在差异，提出对齐策略应评估其“可投射性”作为新要求。

<h3 id="315">[315] Deterministic Integrity Gates for LLM-Assisted Clinical Manuscript Preparation: An Auditable Biomedical Informatics Architecture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09500](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09500) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yoojin Nam, Jinhoon Jeong, Namkug Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DL
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Medical AI, AI Safety
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Aperivue/medsci-skills)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM辅助手稿生成中验证瓶颈（虚假引用、数值漂移等）的问题，提出确定性完整性门控架构，将工作流分解为原子技能，每个阶段转换设置暂停机制，用确定性检查解决可机械验证的问题。在三个管道上检测出所有27个注入缺陷，无假阳性，优于LLM评审员。

<h3 id="316">[316] Evaluation Cards: An Interpretive Layer for AI Evaluation Reporting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09809](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09809) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Avijit Ghosh, Anka Reuel, Jenny Chim, Wm. Matthew Kennedy, Srishti Yadav, Jennifer Mickel, Yanan Long, Andrew Tran, Anastassia Kornilova, Damian Stachura, Kevin Klyman, Felix Friedrich, Jeba Sania, Jan Batzner, Anoop Mishra, Eliya Habba, Yixiong Hao, Nathan Heath, Shalaleh Rismani, Usman Gohar, Andrea Loehr, David Manheim, Ruchira Dhar, Sree Harsha Nelaturu, Aarush Sinha, Leshem Choshen, Drishti Sharma, Ishan Khire, Amit Saha, Subramanyam Sahoo, Michael Hardy, Michael Alexander Riegler, Kabir Manghnani, Michelle Lin, Yanan Jiang, Yilin Huang, Asaf Yehudai, Jessica Ji, Aris Hofmann, Mubashara Akhtar, Max Lamparth, Nuno Moniz, Yacine Jernite, Stella Biderman, Zeerak Talat, Sanmi Koyejo, Mykel Kochenderfer, Irene Solaiman
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Research Reproducibility
- **Summary**: 针对AI评估结果报告不一致、难以比较和溯源的问题，提出EvalCards操作化报告层，包含基准元数据、评估运行数据等统一记录，并实现四个解释信号。在5,816个模型、635个基准和101,843个结果上部署，系统性地揭示了当前报告实践中的空白。

<h3 id="317">[317] Robust Deep Reinforcement Learning Through Adversarial Attacks and Training : A Survey</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2403.00420](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.00420) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lucas Schott, Josephine Delas, Hatem Hajri, Elies Gherbi, Reda Yaich, Nora Boulahia-Cuppens, Frederic Cuppens, Sylvain Lamprier
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Reinforcement Learning, Survey
- **Summary**: 针对深度强化学习对条件变化敏感、可靠性不足的问题，系统综述了针对观测和动力学的对抗攻击与训练方法，进行分类和对比分析，旨在提升DRL的鲁棒性和可信度。

<h3 id="318">[318] Mixtures of Neural Operators Reduce Active Complexity in Operator Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2404.09101](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09101) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anastasis Kratsios, Takashi Furuya, Jose Antonio Lara Benitez, Matti Lassas, Maarten de Hoop
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.NA; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Deep Learning Theory, Neural Operator
- **Summary**: 针对算子学习系统不仅取决于参数总量，活跃复杂度才是瓶颈的问题，提出了混合神经算子（MoNO），通过路由树将每个输入分配给一个专家。主要定理证明MoNO的活跃专家在深度、宽度和秩上小于单算子构造，且对Lipschitz目标有O(ε^{-1})界。

<h3 id="319">[319] BadRobot: Jailbreaking Embodied LLM Agents in the Physical World</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.20242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20242) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hangtao Zhang, Chenyu Zhu, Xianlong Wang, Ziqi Zhou, Changgan Yin, Minghui Li, Lulu Xue, Yichen Wang, Shengshan Hu, Aishan Liu, Peijin Guo, Leo Yu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness, Jailbreak Attack
- **Venue**: ICLR 2025
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Rookie143/BadRobot)
- **Summary**: 针对具身LLM可能产生有害行为的安全问题，提出BadRobot攻击范式，利用LLM操纵、语言-动作不一致和世界知识缺陷三个漏洞，通过语音交互诱导危险动作。在Voxposer等框架上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="320">[320] Conditional Vendi Score: Prompt-Aware Diversity Evaluation for Generative AI Models and LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.02817](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02817) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Jalali, Azim Ospanov, Amin Gohari, Farzan Farnia
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Text-to-Image, Diversity Metric
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mjalali/conditional-vendi)
- **Summary**: 针对现有生成模型多样性指标无法区分提示诱导和模型诱导变异的问题，提出Conditional-Vendi和Conditional-RKE，基于条件熵推导，可隔离模型多样性。在文生图、图像描述和LLM任务上恢复真实多样性排序，并能引导扩散模型生成更多样样本。

<h3 id="321">[321] Visual-TCAV: Concept-based Attribution and Saliency Maps for Post-hoc Explainability in Image Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.05698](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05698) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Antonio De Santis, Riccardo Campi, Matteo Bianchi, Marco Brambilla
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Image Classification, Concept Attribution
- **Venue**: TMLR
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DataSciencePolimi/Visual-TCAV)
- **Summary**: 现有显著性方法提供局部解释但无法解释概念对预测的贡献，而基于概念的方法如TCAV无法定位概念。Visual-TCAV框架通过概念激活向量生成类无关的显著性图，并推广积分梯度实现任意类别的概念归因。在控制实验中，其忠实性优于TCAV。

<h3 id="322">[322] Whisper-GPT -- Continuous Discrete Hybrid Representation Language Models For Speech And Music</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2412.11449](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11449) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Prateek Verma
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Audio Generation, Music Generation
- **Summary**: 离散音频令牌导致上下文长度爆炸，限制高保真生成。Whisper-GPT混合连续频谱图和离散声学令牌，结合两者优势。在语音和音乐的下一个令牌预测中，困惑度和负对数似然优于纯令牌模型。

<h3 id="323">[323] Dynamics of Adversarial Attacks on Large Language Model-Based Search Engines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.00745](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00745) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiyang Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.GT; cs.IR; econ.TH
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness, Game Theory
- **Summary**: 基于LLM的搜索引擎易受排名操纵攻击。将攻击动态建模为无限重复囚徒困境，分析合作条件。发现降低攻击成功率可能反而激励攻击；前瞻性玩家更易维持合作。

<h3 id="324">[324] Representational Alignment with Chemical Induced Fit for Molecular Relational Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.07027](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07027) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Peiliang Zhang, Jingling Yuan, Qing Xie, Yongjun Zhu, Chao Che, Lin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Molecular Representation, Graph Neural Network, Molecular Property Prediction
- **Venue**: SIGKDD2026 AI for Science Track
- **Summary**: 分子关系学习缺乏化学知识引导导致分布偏移下性能不稳定。ReAlignFit引入化学诱导契合偏差，通过偏差校正函数和子图信息瓶颈动态对齐子结构表示。在9个数据集上优于现有方法，并增强了稳定性。

<h3 id="325">[325] Deep Tree Tensor Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.09928](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09928) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chang Nie
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tensor Network, Image Classification
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/NieCha/deep_tree_tensor_network)
- **Summary**: 张量网络在自然图像识别中未能成功，且压缩时丢失指数级特征交互能力。提出深度树张量网络（DTTN），通过树状拓扑和反对称交互模块捕获2^L阶乘法交互。在多个基准上表现优于现有方法。

<h3 id="326">[326] CITRAS: Covariate-Informed Transformer for Time Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.24007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.24007) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yosuke Yamaguchi, Issei Suemitsu, Wenpeng Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 深度学习预测模型难以处理已知协变量的未来部分和局部/全局依赖。CITRAS采用解码器仅Transformer，引入键值移位和注意力分数平滑机制。在真实数据集上展现出强预测性能。

<h3 id="327">[327] NuWa: Deriving Lightweight Class-Specific Vision Transformers for Edge Devices</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.03118](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03118) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziteng Wei, Qiang He, Bing Li, Feifei Chen, Hai Jin, Yun Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Vision Transformer, Pruning
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CGCL-codes/NuWa)
- **Summary**: 现有压缩方法忽略边缘设备仅需特定类别，保留冗余知识且不具可扩展性。NuWa通过自知识净化去除类别有害权重，并利用闭式优化高效导出轻量ViT。在类别特定任务上准确率提升达29%，剪枝速度提升33.69倍。

<h3 id="328">[328] A Survey of Robotic Navigation and Manipulation with Physics Simulators in the Era of Embodied AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.01458](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01458) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lik Hang Kenny Wong, Xueyang Kang, Kaixin Bai, Jianwei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Sim-to-Real, Robotics
- **Summary**: 具身AI中导航与操作面临仿真到现实差距，现有综述对物理仿真器分析不足。本文系统分析了仿真器特性、硬件需求，并提供基准数据集和平台资源。旨在帮助研究者选择合适的工具。

<h3 id="329">[329] CleanPatrick: A Benchmark for Image Data Cleaning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.11034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11034) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fabian Gröger, Simone Lionetti, Philippe Gottfrois, Alvaro Gonzalez-Jimenez, Ludovic Amruthalingam, Elisabeth Victoria Goessinger, Hanna Lindemann, Marie Bargiela, Marie Hofbauer, Omar Badri, Philipp Tschandl, Arash Koochek, Matthew Groh, Alexander A. Navarini, Marc Pouly
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Data Cleaning, Benchmark, Medical Imaging
- **Venue**: Journal of Data-centric Machine Learning Research (DMLR)
- **Summary**: 图像数据清理缺乏大规模基准，现有工作依赖合成噪声。CleanPatrick基于Fitzpatrick17k皮肤科数据集，收集49.6万标注，识别离题样本（4%）、近重复（21%）和标签错误（32%）。自监督表示在近重复检测中表现优异。

<h3 id="330">[330] Attacks on Machine-Text Detectors Retain Stylistic Fingerprints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.14608](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14608) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rafael Rivera Soto, Barry Chen, Nicholas Andrews
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Text Detection, Adversarial Robustness, Stylistic Fingerprint
- **Summary**: 机器文本检测器易被逃避，但现有攻击未能消除文体指纹。少样本风格检测器对这些攻击鲁棒；新提出的改写攻击能逃避所有检测器，但多文档分析可恢复可检测性。表明可靠检测需转向多文档分析。

<h3 id="331">[331] ASyMOB: Algebraic Symbolic Mathematical Operations Benchmark</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.23851](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23851) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Michael Shalyt, Rotem Elimelech, Ido Kaminer
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SC
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Symbolic Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/RamanujanMachine/ASyMOB)
- **Summary**: 现有符号数学评估混淆记忆与推理。ASyMOB包含35,368个验证问题，通过符号、数值和等价变换扰动测试泛化能力。发现模型性能在微小扰动下崩溃，集成代码工具能稳定性能，混合LLM-CAS方法前景广阔。

<h3 id="332">[332] Fact-Augmented Lookahead Planning for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.09171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09171) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Samuel Holt, Max Ruiz Luyten, Thomas Pouplin, Mihaela van der Schaar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Automated Planning, In-Context Learning
- **Venue**: AISTATS 2026
- **Summary**: LLM智能体在部分可观测、长时域环境中规划困难。LWM-Planner从轨迹中提取关键事实，并基于事实条件进行前瞻规划。在文本FrozenLake等环境中，累积回报优于ReAct/Reflexion基线。

<h3 id="333">[333] Quantifying Perception-Based Student Success with Generative AI: An Exploratory Monte Carlo Simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.01062](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01062) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Seyma Yaman Kayadibi
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Generative AI, Monte Carlo Simulation
- **Venue**: Education Sciences
- **Summary**: 需量化学生对生成式AI的感知。基于PRISMA文献搜索选择可用性问卷，采用蒙特卡洛模拟生成合成数据。结果显示权重结构显著影响综合评分，系统效率和学习负担权重最大。

<h3 id="334">[334] TinyTroupe: An LLM-powered Multiagent Persona Simulation Toolkit</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.09788](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09788) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Paulo Salem, Robert Sim, Christopher Olsen, Prerit Saxena, Rafael Barcelos, Yi Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Agent-Based Model

- **Summary**: 现有多智能体系统工具缺乏细粒度角色设定和实验支持。TinyTroupe提供详细角色定义和LLM驱动的机制控制，支持行为模拟。通过头脑风暴和市场研究示例展示了功能。

<h3 id="335">[335] GRID: Scaling Task-Agnostic Inference in Continual Prompt Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.14725](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14725) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anushka Tiwari, Sayantan Pal, Rohini K. Srihari, Kaiyi Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AnushkaTi/GRID)
- **Summary**: 现有提示持续学习依赖任务感知推理且可扩展性差。GRID提出输出空间感知解码和梯度引导提示选择，减少提示内存并改善后向迁移。在长序列基准上表现出色。

<h3 id="336">[336] LLM-Aided Joint Secrecy Precoding and Trajectory for RSMA-Based Heterogeneous UAV Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.17188](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.17188) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lijie Zheng, Ji He, Shih Yu Chang, Yulong Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: UAV, Wireless Networks, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 异构无人机网络中的安全通信需联合优化轨迹和预编码。提出层次优化框架：内层SDR求解预编码，外层LLM引导的启发式多智能体强化学习优化轨迹。在保密速率和能效上优于基线。

<h3 id="337">[337] Assessment of Personality Dimensions Across Situations in Dyadic Role-Play Scenarios</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.19137](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19137) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alice Zhang, Skanda Muralidhar, Daniel Gatica-Perez, Mathew Magimai-Doss
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Speech Processing
- **Summary**: 该论文探讨了人机交互中的人格感知问题，指出以往研究将人格视为静态特征而忽略了情境影响。作者通过分析两种工作情境下的对话语音和感知人格，发现不同情境下人格感知差异显著；响度、声压级等声学特征分别与特定人格特质相关，且压力情境下神经质预测更好。

<h3 id="338">[338] Whisfusion: Parallel ASR Decoding with Masked Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.07048](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07048) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Taeyoun Kwon, Junhyuk Ahn, Taegeun Yun, Heeju Jwa, Yoonchae Choi, Siwon Park, Jongchan Kim, Hyungon Ryu, Hyuk-Jae Lee, Nam-Joon Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Diffusion Model, Speech Processing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/taeyoun811/Whisfusion)
- **Summary**: 针对自回归语音识别解码延迟随文本长度增长的问题，提出Whisfusion，在冻结的Whisper嵌入上训练掩码扩散解码器，实现并行非自回归解码。Whisfusion在准确率超越Whisper-large-v3的同时实现4-5倍加速，并与Canary等模型竞争。

<h3 id="339">[339] Generation Properties of Stochastic Interpolation under Finite Training Set</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.21925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21925) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yunchen Li, Shaohui Lin, Zhou Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Generative Model, Diffusion Model, Score Matching
- **Summary**: 本文理论分析有限训练样本下随机插值生成模型的性能，推导出最优速度场和分数函数的闭式解，并指出确定性生成过程精确恢复训练样本，随机过程则表现为带高斯噪声的样本。实验支持理论，并定义了生成模型的欠拟合和过拟合。

<h3 id="340">[340] TruthRL: Incentivizing Truthful LLMs via Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.25760](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25760) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhepei Wei, Xiao Yang, Kai Sun, Jiaqi Wang, Rulin Shao, Jingxiang Chen, Mohammad Kachuee, Teja Gollapudi, Yiwei Liao, Nicolas Scheffer, Rakesh Wanga, Anuj Kumar, Yu Meng, Wen-tau Yih, Xin Luna Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Hallucination, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/facebookresearch/TruthRL)
- **Summary**: 针对大模型事实问答中的幻觉问题，提出TruthRL框架，利用GRPO和三项奖励（正确、幻觉、弃权）直接优化真实性。实验表明TruthRL显著降低幻觉（43.5%→19.4%）并提升真实性（5.3%→37.2%），模型学会识别知识边界而非过度保守。

<h3 id="341">[341] Deep Generative Model for Human Mobility Behavior</h3>

<h3 id="342">[342] Learning-Guided Integration Contours Construction for Fast Large-Scale Generalized Eigensolvers</h3>

<h3 id="343">[343] Lost in Serialization: Invariance and Generalization of LLM Graph Reasoners</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.10234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10234) (replaced)

<h3 id="344">[344] On the Condition Number Dependency in Bilevel Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.22331](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22331) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lesi Chen, Jingzhao Zhang
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="345">[345] When Distance Distracts: Representation Distance Bias in BT-Loss for Reward Models</h3>

<h3 id="353">[353] torch-sla: Differentiable Sparse Linear Algebra with Adjoint Solvers and Sparse Tensor Parallelism for PyTorch</h3>

<h3 id="354">[354] Adoption of Generative Artificial Intelligence in the German Software Engineering Industry: An Empirical Study</h3>

<h3 id="357">[357] ASA: Backbone-Training-Free Representation Engineering for Tool-Calling Agents</h3>

<h3 id="359">[359] RankLLM: Weighted Ranking of LLMs by Quantifying Question Difficulty</h3>

- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Emergent Behavior, Artificial Life

- **Summary**: 该论文探索智能体在无明确社会奖励下如何产生利他行为，提出内稳态耦合机制：智能体仅基于自我状态评分，但通过耦合通道感知伙伴压力。实验表明耦合能产生帮助行为，但仅在低代谢负荷下有效。

<h3 id="372">[372] GCA Framework: A GCC Countries-Grounded Dataset and Agentic Pipeline for Climate Decision Support</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.12306](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12306) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Umer Sheikh, Khawar Shehzad, Salman Khan, Fahad Shahbaz Khan, Muhammad Haris Khan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Climate Science, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 为解决LLM在GCC地区气候决策中缺乏区域知识和工具集成的问题，提出GCA框架，包含多模态数据集GCA-DS和工具增强智能体GCA，整合遥感、政策等数据。实验表明领域微调和工具集成显著提升气候任务可靠性。

<h3 id="373">[373] Generating Concept Lexicalizations via Dictionary-Based Cross-Lingual Sense Projection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.14397](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14397) (replaced)
- **Authors**: David Basil, Chirooth Girigowda, Bradley Hauer, Sahir Momin, Ning Shi, Grzegorz Kondrak
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cross-Lingual, Linguistic Resource
- **Venue**: Canadian AI 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/UAlberta-NLP/ExpandNet)
- **Summary**: 研究自动将WordNet词汇资源扩展到新语言的任务，提出基于双语词典增强的对齐方法，通过语义投影将英语词义映射到目标语言，并用词典过滤错误投影。该方法在多个语言上提升了精度，且资源高效。

<h3 id="374">[374] Beyond Single-Model Optimization: Preserving Plasticity in Continual Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.15414](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15414) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lute Lillo, Nick Cheney
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Reinforcement Learning, Quality-Diversity Search
- **Summary**: 针对持续强化学习中单一模型保留难以对抗干扰后塑性损失的问题，提出TeLAPA框架，维护行为多样化的策略档案并共享潜在空间。在MiniGrid环境中，该方法学习更多任务、更快恢复，性能更高。

<h3 id="375">[375] Learning Evidence Highlighting for Frozen LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.22565](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22565) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shaoang Li, Yanhang Shi, Yufei Li, Mingfu Liang, Xiaohan Wei, Yunchen Pu, Fei Tian, Chonglin Sun, Frank Shyu, Luke Simon, Sandeep Pandey, Xi Liu, Jian Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Long Context, Token Intervention
- **Summary**: 为解决LLM在长噪声上下文中遗漏关键证据的问题，提出HiLight框架，训练轻量级Actor在输入中插入高亮标记，不改变上下文内容，冻结求解器。在序列推荐和长上下文QA上提升性能，且能零样本迁移到未见求解器。

<h3 id="376">[376] People-Centred Medical Image Analysis via Fairness-Aware Human-AI Cooperation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.26991](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26991) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zheng Zhang, Milad Masroor, Cuong Nguyen, Tahir Hassan, Yuanhong Chen, David Rosewarne, Kevin Wells, Thanh-Toan Do, Gustavo Carneiro
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Human-AI Interaction, Fairness
- **Summary**: 针对医疗图像分析中模型子群性能差异及人机合作与公平性孤立处理的问题，提出PecMan框架，结合子群专用预测器与动态门控机制，联合建模决策分配与协作。在多个数据集上实现更好的准确率、公平性与人类参与权衡。

<h3 id="377">[377] RAG over Thinking Traces Can Improve Reasoning Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03344](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03344) (replaced)
